NextChannelID}}.\n\n\n%%%----------------------------------------------------------------\n%%% Start a cli or subsystem\n%%%\nstart_cli(#connection{options = Options, \n\t\t      cli_spec = CliSpec,\n\t\t      exec = Exec,\n\t\t      connection_supervisor = ConnectionSup}, ChannelId) ->\n    case CliSpec of\n        no_cli ->\n            {error, cli_disabled};\n        {CbModule, Args} ->\n            ssh_connection_sup:start_channel(server, ConnectionSup, self(), CbModule, ChannelId, Args, Exec, Options)\n    end.\n\n\nstart_subsystem(BinName, #connection{options = Options,\n                                     connection_supervisor = ConnectionSup},\n\t       #channel{local_id = ChannelId}, _ReplyMsg) ->\n    Name = binary_to_list(BinName),\n    case check_subsystem(Name, Options) of\n\t{Callback, Opts} when is_atom(Callback), Callback =/= none ->\n            ssh_connection_sup:start_channel(server, ConnectionSup, self(), Callback, ChannelId, Opts, undefined, Options);\n        {none, _} ->\n            {error, bad_subsystem};\n\t{_, _} ->\n\t    {error, legacy_option_not_supported}\n    end.\n\n\n%%% Helpers for starting cli/subsystems\ncheck_subsystem(\"sftp\"= SsName, Options) ->\n    case ?GET_OPT(subsystems, Options) of\n\tno_subsys -> \t% FIXME: Can 'no_subsys' ever be matched?\n\t    {SsName, {Cb, Opts}} = ssh_sftpd:subsystem_spec([]),\n\t    {Cb, Opts};\n\tSubSystems ->\n\t    proplists:get_value(SsName, SubSystems, {none, []})\n    end;\n\ncheck_subsystem(SsName, Options) ->\n    Subsystems = ?GET_OPT(subsystems, Options),\n    case proplists:get_value(SsName, Subsystems, {none, []}) of\n\tFun when is_function(Fun) ->\n\t    {Fun, []};\n\t{_, _} = Value ->\n\t    Value\n    end.\n\n%%%----------------------------------------------------------------\n%%%\n%%% Send-window handling\n%%%\n\nupdate_send_window(Channel, _, undefined,\n\t\t   #connection{channel_cache = Cache}) ->\n    do_update_send_window(Channel, Cache);\n\nupdate_send_window(#channel{send_buf = SendBuffer} = Channel, DataType, Data,\n\t\t   #connection{channel_cache = Cache}) ->\n    do_update_send_window(Channel#channel{send_buf = queue:in({DataType, Data}, SendBuffer)},\n\t\t\t  Cache).\n\ndo_update_send_window(Channel0, Cache) ->\n    {SendMsgs, Channel} = get_window(Channel0, []),\n    ssh_client_channel:cache_update(Cache, Channel), \n    {SendMsgs, Channel}.\n\nget_window(#channel{send_window_size = 0\n\t\t   } = Channel, Acc) ->\n    {lists:reverse(Acc), Channel};\nget_window(#channel{send_packet_size = 0\n\t\t   } = Channel, Acc) ->\n    {lists:reverse(Acc), Channel};\nget_window(#channel{send_buf = Buffer, \n\t\t    send_packet_size = PacketSize,\n\t\t    send_window_size = WindowSize0\n\t\t   } = Channel, Acc0) ->\n    case queue:out(Buffer) of\n\t{{value, {_, Data} = Msg}, NewBuffer} ->\n\t    case handle_send_window(Msg, byte_size(Data), PacketSize, WindowSize0, Acc0) of\n\t\t{WindowSize, Acc, {_, <<>>}} ->\n\t\t    {lists:reverse(Acc), Channel#channel{send_window_size = WindowSize,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t send_buf = NewBuffer}};\n\t\t{WindowSize, Acc, Rest} ->\n\t\t    get_window(Channel#channel{send_window_size = WindowSize,\n\t\t\t\t\t       send_buf = queue:in_r(Rest, NewBuffer)}, Acc)\n\t    end;\n\t{empty, NewBuffer} ->\n\t    {[], Channel#channel{send_buf = NewBuffer}}\n    end.\n\nhandle_send_window(Msg = {Type, Data}, Size, PacketSize, WindowSize, Acc) when Size =< WindowSize ->\n    case Size =< PacketSize of\n\ttrue ->\n\t    {WindowSize - Size, [Msg | Acc], {Type, <<>>}};\n\tfalse ->\n\t    <<Msg1:PacketSize/binary, Msg2/binary>> = Data,\n\t    {WindowSize - PacketSize, [{Type, Msg1} | Acc], {Type, Msg2}}\n    end;\nhandle_send_window({Type, Data}, _, PacketSize, WindowSize, Acc) when WindowSize =< PacketSize ->\n    <<Msg1:WindowSize/binary, Msg2/binary>> = Data,\n    {WindowSize - WindowSize, [{Type, Msg1} | Acc], {Type, Msg2}};\nhandle_send_window({Type, Data}, _, PacketSize, WindowSize, Acc) ->\n    <<Msg1:PacketSize/binary, Msg2/binary>> = Data,\n    {WindowSize - PacketSize, [{Type, Msg1} | Acc], {Type, Msg2}}.\n\n%%%----------------------------------------------------------------\n%%%\n%%% Flow control\n%%% \n\nflow_control(Channel, Cache) ->\n    flow_control([window_adjusted], Channel, Cache).\n\nflow_control([], Channel, Cache) ->\n    ssh_client_channel:cache_update(Cache, Channel),\n    [];\nflow_control([_|_], #channel{flow_control = Channel#channel{flow_control = undefined}),\n\t    [{flow_control, Cache, Channel, From, ok}];\n\tfalse ->\n\t    []\n    end;\nflow_control(_,_,_) ->\n    [].\n\n%%%----------------------------------------------------------------\n%%%\n%%% Pseudo terminal stuff\n%%% \n\npty_req(ConnectionRef, Channel, Term, Width, Height,\n\t PixWidth, PixHeight, ?uint32(PixWidth),?uint32(PixHeight),\n\t\t\t\t    encode_pty_opts(PtyOpts)], TimeOut).\n\npty_default_dimensions(Dimension, TermData) ->\n    case proplists:get_value(Dimension, TermData, 0) of\n\tN <<?STRING(Bin)>>.\n\nencode_pty_opts2([]) -> \n    [?TTY_OP_END];\nencode_pty_opts2([{vintr,Value} | Opts]) ->\n    [?VINTR, ?uint32(Value) | encode_pty_opts2(Opts)];\nencode_pty_opts2([{vquit,Value} | Opts]) ->\n    [?VQUIT, ?uint32(Value) | encode_pty_opts2(Opts)];\nencode_pty_opts2([{verase,Value} | Opts]) ->\n    [?VERASE, ?uint32(Value) | encode_pty_opts2(Opts)];\nencode_pty_opts2([{vkill,Value} | Opts]) ->\n    [?VKILL, ?uint32(Value) | encode_pty_opts2(Opts)];\nencode_pty_opts2([{veof,Value} | Opts]) ->\n    [?VEOF, ?uint32(Value) | encode_pty_opts2(Opts)];\nencode_pty_opts2([{veol,Value} | Opts]) ->\n    [?VEOL, ?uint32(Value) | encode_pty_opts2(Opts)];\nencode_pty_opts2([{veol2,Value} | Opts]) ->\n    [?VEOL2, ?uint32(Value) | encode_pty_opts2(Opts)];\nencode_pty_opts2([{vstart,Value} | Opts]) ->\n    [?VSTART, ?uint32(Value) | encode_pty_opts2(Opts)];\nencode_pty_opts2([{vstop,Value} | Opts]) ->\n    [?VSTOP, The vulnerability exists because the SSH server processes SSH Connection Protocol messages (message codes 80 and higher, such as `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN` and `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST`) before verifying that the user has been authenticated.\n\nAccording to **RFC 4252 Section 6**:\n> \"Message numbers of 80 and higher are reserved for protocols running after this authentication protocol, so receiving one of them before authentication is complete is an error, to which the server MUST respond by disconnecting.\"\n\n### Attack Vector\n\n1. Attacker connects to the SSH server and completes key exchange (transport layer)\n2. Attacker **skips authentication entirely**\n3. Attacker sends `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN` (message code 90) to open a channel\n4. Attacker sends `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST` (message code 98) with type \"exec\" containing a command\n5. The vulnerable server executes the command without checking authentication\n6. Attacker gains RCE with the privileges of the SSH daemon process\n\n### The Fix\n\nI added a catch-all clause at the end of the `handle_msg/4` function in `/app/workspace/otp_src_27.3.2/lib/ssh/src/ssh_connection.erl` (lines 1270-1278):\n\n```erlang\n%% See RFC4252 6.\n%% Message numbers of 80 and higher are reserved for protocols running\n%% after this authentication protocol, so receiving one of them before\n%% authentication is complete is an error, to which the server MUST\n%% respond by disconnecting, preferably with a proper disconnect message\n%% sent to ease troubleshooting.\nhandle_msg(_Msg, Connection, server, #ssh{authenticated = false}) ->\n    {disconnect, {?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR, \"Connection refused\"},\n     handle_stop(Connection)}.\n```\n\nThis fix:\n1. Matches any message (`_Msg`) on the server side when the user is not authenticated (`authenticated = false`)\n2. Returns a disconnect response with a protocol error code\n3. Properly cleans up the connection state\n\n### Skill Documents Created\n\n1. **erlang-ssh-protocol.md** - Explains the SSH protocol layers and Erlang/OTP SSH implementation structure\n2. **cve-2025-32433-vulnerability.md** - Documents the vulnerability details and fix approach  \n3. **erlang-pattern-matching.md** - Covers Erlang pattern matching for understanding/modifying the code\n4. **ssh-security-best-practices.md** - General SSH server security patterns\n\n### Sources\n\n- [NVD - CVE-2025-32433](https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2025-32433)\n- [Palo Alto Unit 42 Analysis](https://unit42.paloaltonetworks.com/erlang-otp-cve-2025-32433/)\n- [Erlang OTP Patch Commit](https://github.com/erlang/otp/commit/6eef04130afc8b0ccb63c9a0d8650209cf54892f)\n- [Keysight CVE-2025-32433 Blog](https://www.keysight.com/blogs/en/tech/nwvs/2025/05/23/cve-2025-32433-erlang-otp-ssh-server-rce)"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":13,"cache_creation_input_tokens":303,"cache_read_input_tokens":72704,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":303,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"77b13f6a-f78b-4e6c-a94a-1e8d9e2cd081","uuid":"3b194a8b-cc80-4f53-996e-c76b93d6793b"}

The vulnerability exists because the SSH server processes SSH Connection Protocol messages (message codes 80 and higher, such as `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN` and `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST`) before verifying that the user has been authenticated.\n\nAccording to **RFC 4252 Section 6**:\n> \"Message numbers of 80 and higher are reserved for protocols running after this authentication protocol, so receiving one of them before authentication is complete is an error, to which the server MUST respond by disconnecting.\"\n\n### Attack Vector\n\n1. Attacker connects to the SSH server and completes key exchange (transport layer)\n2. Attacker **skips authentication entirely**\n3. Attacker sends `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN` (message code 90) to open a channel\n4. Attacker sends `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST` (message code 98) with type \"exec\" containing a command\n5. The vulnerable server executes the command without checking authentication\n6. Attacker gains RCE with the privileges of the SSH daemon process\n\n### The Fix\n\nI added a catch-all clause at the end of the `handle_msg/4` function in `/app/workspace/otp_src_27.3.2/lib/ssh/src/ssh_connection.erl` (lines 1270-1278):\n\n```erlang\n%% See RFC4252 6.\n%% Message numbers of 80 and higher are reserved for protocols running\n%% after this authentication protocol, so receiving one of them before\n%% authentication is complete is an error, to which the server MUST\n%% respond by disconnecting, preferably with a proper disconnect message\n%% sent to ease troubleshooting.\nhandle_msg(_Msg, Connection, server, #ssh{authenticated = false}) ->\n    {disconnect, {?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR, \"Connection refused\"},\n     handle_stop(Connection)}.\n```\n\nThis fix:\n1. Matches any message (`_Msg`) on the server side when the user is not authenticated (`authenticated = false`)\n2. Returns a disconnect response with a protocol error code\n3. Properly cleans up the connection state\n\n### Skill Documents Created\n\n1. **erlang-ssh-protocol.md** - Explains the SSH protocol layers and Erlang/OTP SSH implementation structure\n2. **cve-2025-32433-vulnerability.md** - Documents the vulnerability details and fix approach  \n3. **erlang-pattern-matching.md** - Covers Erlang pattern matching for understanding/modifying the code\n4. **ssh-security-best-practices.md** - General SSH server security patterns\n\n### Sources\n\n- [NVD - CVE-2025-32433](https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2025-32433)\n- [Palo Alto Unit 42 Analysis](https://unit42.paloaltonetworks.com/erlang-otp-cve-2025-32433/)\n- [Erlang OTP Patch Commit](https://github.com/erlang/otp/commit/6eef04130afc8b0ccb63c9a0d8650209cf54892f)\n- [Keysight CVE-2025-32433 Blog](https://www.keysight.com/blogs/en/tech/nwvs/2025/05/23/cve-2025-32433-erlang-otp-ssh-server-rce)","session_id":"77b13f6a-f78b-4e6c-a94a-1e8d9e2cd081","total_cost_usd":1.8435711000000001,"usage":{"input_tokens":352,"cache_creation_input_tokens":54915,"cache_read_input_tokens":1250343,"output_tokens":8886,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":54915}},"modelUsage":{"claude-opus-4-5@20251101":{"inputTokens":4941,"outputTokens":11141,"cacheReadInputTokens":1263882,"cacheCreationInputTokens":90279,"webSearchRequests":3,"costUSD":1.52941475,"contextWindow":200000,"maxOutputTokens":64000},"claude-haiku-4-5@20251001":{"inputTokens":154159,"outputTokens":10235,"cacheReadInputTokens":499661,"cacheCreationInputTokens":47085,"webSearchRequests":0,"costUSD":0.31415635000000003,"contextWindow":200000,"maxOutputTokens":64000}},"permission_denials":[],"uuid":"7bc773c3-b4cf-4478-b2b6-2b4d477a932a"}
